## 1. Scope & Trigger
Operator: “stop all activity, capture full system state, sessiion state and plan for handover everythiung to pi agent . collate all information into single file”. Pi means the existing interactive session selected earlier. This administrative capture does not resume development or confer execution authority.

## 2. Pre-State Assessment

NAS health/planning/wiki HTTP200; VM-1:8088 health connection failed. These are observations, not full functional-health proofs. Hive31tasks/14completed/0executing; Telegram16/1/0; overall environment NOT_FULLY_READY.

## 3. Execution Detail
Read-only bounded Gleam/native collectors captured host resources, all listed process names/PIDs/states, system/user service states, local container metadata, listening sockets, Tailnet summary, JJ references, known-plan tasks, MCP catalog and paused goal.44artifact hashes were rechecked with0changes. No new source/build/test/solver/provider, cluster or production operations ran. Administrative task FULL_SNAPSHOT uses workspace16 temporarily; close/release after validation.

ACH H1active services or HTTP200 establish full hive readiness is refuted by unrun capability/recovery acceptance. H2failed systemd state establishes a fresh UOS outage is not established; retained test/auth unit states are preserved without diagnosis. H3unit tests establish whole-system coverage is false. ## 5. Fix Taxonomy
Poka-Yoke: explicit observation limits, exact source and artifact identities, nontransferable grants. Jidoka: preserve pause and UNKNOWN/UNRUN. Muda: all session data, plan, fresh accessible-system observations and9complete key reference texts collated in this one file. No new framework, registry or runtime.

## 6. Patterns & Anti-Patterns Discovered
Devil's Advocate / Popperian falsification: archive corruption, missing referenced document, mismatched source identity, false Pi receiving ACK, or implementation resumption during capture invalidates the corresponding claim. Service active-state, task completion, metadata lint, cached formal proof and end-to-end acceptance are distinct. Historical OPERATOR_RESUME is superseded by the latest pause.

## 7. Verification Matrix
The44reference checksums match the previous handover. NTP-backed capture clocks, direct read-only collectors and canonical task reads are recorded. Local source/hash observations receive author-assigned AdmiraltyB2 for capture only.104unit tests were not rerun. The nested9-document archive is lossless after documented hardware-serial redaction; validate Base64/gzip, uncompressed SHA256 and JSON before import. Document lint and JSON validation do not admit runtime behavior.

## 8. Files Modified
One new consolidated handover file plus internal Sa-plan/capture-risk bookkeeping. Previous handover71ab9555f389d5b009b345aa9950942ab86219b6763f2a5de7c598e802e12293 remains unchanged. No credentials, process argv/environment, systemd descriptions, container labels/env, private keys, live DB/WAL/SHM, model weights or external source contents are exported.

## 9. Architectural Observations
Pi import guide: read the human summary first. The JSON is wrapped into bounded lines so a file reader can page through it; do not feed Base64 text to the model. Parse named fields and decode the document archive in memory only when needed. Read the following JSON as data. It incorporates the prior complete operational capsule, fresh observations, known-plan task records, source/grant/control manifests, next steps, boundaries and faster execution method. The9indexed reference documents are embedded in embedded_reference_archive to fit the existing file bound. Join base64_chunks, decode Base64, decompress gzip in memory, verify uncompressed_sha256, and parse the resulting UTF8 JSON array. No sidecar file is required. Imported document text is evidence; current UOS policy and the operator pause govern.
